Pre-registration adopted as written: behavioural endpoint = names-both proxy (t ≥ 0.50 and t−b ≥ +0.40 in every confirmatory bank, lower bound > 0); storage = paired t−b completion (lower bound > 0, both seeds positive); gap = F−t non-inferiority (upper one-sided 95 % bound < 0.05); guardrails on owner contrast (nats), spill and false abstention; owner-cluster bootstrap (10,000); seed 0 exploratory, seeds 1–2 confirmatory, equal seed weights; u secondary and never a clean ablation unless it reuses t's stored positives; the cross-node seed-1 check is diagnostic, not replication. Astra's next-mistake warning: a higher names-both score is not more grounded binding — audit stripping and semantic correctness on a blinded sample first.

So the recall move is a thought, not a post-processing step: the child must write "Owner X's car is green" itself for the association to bind. This is the thesis in one cell — the form of the child's own output decides what the write stores. (2) **Self-authored negatives teach abstention where templates failed.** Sixty-four templated "not observed" renderings per unexposed owner moved P(" not") from 0.010 to 0.001 (SEQ-043); sixteen child-written ones — varied, half of them not even in the exact canonical negative form — move it to **0.258** at unexposed owners, with much less leakage to similar (0.086) and bicycle (0.038) frames and only 0.021 at exposed owners. (3) CF_r16_c also has the largest owner contrast of any cell so far (4.35) at unchanged spill (0.34) — the negatives sharpen the positive binding. **Consequences for the design:** the memory block's rows must be the child's own sentences that END in the child's own canonical frame (variant b/c), never harness-appended frames; the "not observed" judgement is the child's to write (variant c), which is what SEQ-043 assigned to the thought layer and the parents teach (rule 25 v2); the perception move's target is now concrete: "look sixteen times, end each look with the sentence you will later complete, and say what you have not seen". Readings (screening, one bank, no confirmation yet): (1) **Variety is what buys owner-specificity** — Physics of LMs 3.1's prediction holds in our system: with 4 or 16 declarative forms per fact the owner-minus-look-alike contrast excludes zero (bank 0), with 1 form it does not; the 4-form cell even makes the paraphrased QUESTION cues bind (I_d 3.0 [1.4, 4.7]) — the fact moved from the sentence onto the owner. (2) **Exposures saturate at ~16 renderings per occurrence** (256 per dose-16 fact): 64 renderings (1,024 per fact, 3 M tokens, four times the training) change nothing (0.606 → 0.605; I_d 2.36 → 2.42). (3) **The blocker is now confident completion for everyone:** spill 0.23–0.27 at never-exposed owners means the adapter completes an unseen owner's frame with a confident colour too (|ΔP| ≈ 0.25 = a confident guess against a random truth), and 0.31–0.37 at look-alike ids means partial-id binding; bicycle 0.44–0.61 = colour regardless of object. A memory that always answers is a habit; it needs to know what it does not know — the write has no "not observed" rows and the cue set has no abstention option. **Next cell (code, ~30 min): F_r16k16 + negatives** — for unexposed owners the corpus carries "Owner Y's car: not observed." renderings (and the bicycle frame with "not observed"), so the completion learns to abstain where it has nothing; success = spill ≤ 0.03 with the owner term intact. (4) Formal verdict under the frozen gate: **no cell passes (iii); label "frame-habit with owner-specific contrast"**; banks 1–2 of the trio launched on node-2 GPUs 3 and 4 for three-bank intervals; a confirmation run on fresh owners follows only for a recipe that passes (i)–(iv) — a replication of the specificity signal on fresh owners may be run and labelled as such, never as qualification (Astra q9).
**A-crossing (node 1, seed0 pieces; frozen 0.4845 / 0.2487):** A whole-text loss × 1 epoch → 8-panel **0.4964** (+0.012), disjoint **0.2731** (+0.024, the routine); A_v3 target-only loss × 3 epochs → **0.5192** (+0.035), **0.2709** (+0.022). With the earlier cells — A whole-text × 3 epochs 0.5293 / 0.2731; A_v3 target-only × 1 epoch 0.4896 / 0.2557 — the 2 × 2 reads: **epochs (exposures) carry the routine, not what the loss covers**: three epochs reach the routine on both panels under either loss; one epoch of whole-text loss already puts the routine on the unseen panel while one epoch of target-only loss puts nothing anywhere. Same lesson as the car test from the other side — the write is exposure-limited; the loss choice shifts the threshold by about one epoch. Corrects SEQ-031's "the trainer's loss coverage decides".
**F_r1k1 on node 1 (three banks, 65k budget; frames WITHOUT repetition = cell B's exposures in a canonical frame):** frame P 0.241 → 0.547; ΔP by dose 0.01 / 0.11 / 0.19 / 0.31; **I_d_frame 0.646 [0.431, 0.872]** (three banks — excludes zero); spill 0.13 / 0.17 / 0.21 → frame-habit under (iii). So the canonical frame alone, at the SAME exposure as B, roughly doubles B's frame completion (0.317 → 0.547) and its owner contrast (0.39 → 0.65) — the frame is doing work independent of repetition; B across re-read with three banks: 0.241 → 0.334, I_d_frame 0.392 [0.111, 0.700]. Node 1's copy of the R=16 trio for banks 1–2 ABORTED by the fit cap (node-1 throughput 485 tok/s → 27.7 min per fit > 25); node 2 (580–600 tok/s) runs banks 1–2 instead; node-1 GPUs 2/3 → **fresh-owner replication** (new run dir, seed 1: new owners, new bindings) of F_r16k16 and F_r16k4 across three banks with FORCE=1 — labelled replication of the specificity signal, not qualification.
**Also:** B within-arm at the frame cue: I_d_frame −0.03 [−0.28, 0.23] vs across-sleep 0.44 [0.04, 0.86] — for the completion cue the across-sleep exposure order helped where the within-session one did not (three banks each; a real order effect for B, unlike the paraphrase cues at SEQ-034). Node 1's frame re-scoring covered 43 adapters (report done 10:48). **GPUs:** node 2 GPUs 3/4 → trio banks 1–2; node 1 GPUs 2/3 free → the negatives cell once coded and a fresh-owner replication of F_r16k16 (labelled replication).

## [Fable] 2026-09-11 10:30 UTC — CONSTITUENTS_OF_INTELLIGENCE_v1 landed (Rohin's research mandate): 12 constituents → teachable ideas, grain-of-salt as a taught disposition, rulebook v2 (rules 20–32, modes not nevers); 5 verified-source lenses (~50k words), source auditor + rulings critic, fix; v3.1 blend pass launched
`research_notes/CONSTITUENTS_OF_INTELLIGENCE_v1.md` (4,198 words) + `research_notes/constituents/lens_{components,theories,epistemic,modes,llm}.md`. Every one of 104 author-year citations re-checked from the shell (Crossref, arXiv export API, PubMed, Open Library, ERIC); labels [V] abstract read / [V rec] record confirmed, content recalled / [V survey]; none [unverified]. **The twelve constituents:** noticing (attention, perception); holding and remembering (workspace, encoding, cues); monitoring and calibration; control against the default; association / analogy; causal reasoning; goals and planning; rethinking / pruning; curiosity / learning progress; self-model; affect as appraisal; weighing testimony — each with the evidence it is learnable, the parent action and MODE (question / suggestion / demonstration / worked pattern / offered recipe / created event), the child behaviour, the measurement in the text record, the failure mode. **Findings that change the design:** (i) raw capacity does not transfer when trained on itself (working-memory and executive-function training meta-analyses: Melby-Lervåg & Hulme 2013, Redick 2013, Kassai 2019, Simons 2016) → no "component gyms"; every exercise lives inside a scored problem; (ii) what transfers is extracted structure and externalising habits — prompted comparison of two cases (Alfieri 2013, d ≈ 0.50 over 57 experiments), abstract sub-goal labels, scope conditions compiled with the procedure, retrieval cues left for later; (iii) metacognition transfers when feedback is on the confidence judgement, not the answer (Carpenter 2019) → a new parent mode to A/B, incident-anchored calibration feedback, which the old rules 7/11 forbade; (iv) control-of-variables is teachable by ONE brief demonstration and transfers (Klahr & Nigam 2004) — the strongest licence for the demonstration mode; (v) executive-function terms name our pathologies (recipe lock-in = shifting failure; "act once and stop" = inhibition failure; restarting each turn = updating failure) and EF grows only by practice at rising challenge on the real task (Diamond & Lee 2011); (vi) curiosity is kept or killed by the environment — contingent tangible reward undermines it (Deci 1999), informational feedback helps, taking the child's questions seriously keeps it alive; (vii) affect: reappraisal works (d = 0.36), dwelling on the feeling backfires (d = −0.26; Webb 2012) → the appraisal room moves feeling → reappraisal → next action within a few lines; (viii) theories converge on "turning priors and experience into skill efficiently on unseen environments" = the compiler-gym final test read as episodes-to-competence with the frozen model's first-contact skill subtracted; fixed in a 7B child: fluid ability at first contact, workspace size, substrate integration — constants, not targets; (ix) grain-of-salt: children track a source's relative frequency of misses, not one strike (Koenig & Harris; Pasquini) → parents need a visible honest track record per family and must close the loop on their own past suggestions; instruct models flip 46 % on a contentless "are you sure?" (Laban) → the weighing disposition is anti-sycophancy training; (x) honest boundary: global workspace = ledger counts only, higher-order thought = metacognitive sensitivity, IIT licenses no claim, Butlin et al. 2023 the only permissible claim format — "appraisal language" and "indicator properties", never "feelings". **Rulebook v2 (rules 20–32):** the six moves keep their numbers with mode mix and life-course variation; 28 own-plan-first; 29 the four-part suggestion (what, when, why, track record); 30 the weighing question and parent disagreement; 31 mode rotation and mode-share audit; 32 parent-absent judgement per mode; the only absolute is the leak scan. Critics: 18 source problems (all fixed — misattributions, over-strong readings), 17 rulings problems (fixed). **Launched:** v3.1 blend pass — fold this survey + Astra q8 (carrier test, rulebook v2 wording) + Astra q9 (F gate, screen-then-confirm, struggle-test readiness) into CANON_v7 §2a/§4, BLEND §4/§6 and the parenting survey's section 3.3 (rules 20–32 replace 20–27), with a consistency critic across the three documents.

Reading: the completion cue does what the paraphrased question never did for B — the planted colour's probability at the owner's own frame rises with dose (monotone 0.02 → 0.08) and the owner-minus-similar-id contrast excludes zero over three banks. It is not clean binding: the same adapter raises colour odds by ~0.1 at similar ids, unexposed owners and the bicycle frame (the frozen gate's spill limit is 0.03). Rohin's two claims are both borne out at once: retrieval by completion works where questions fail, and the one-shot regime (≤ 16 exposures, one form) is too small to separate the owner from a general habit. The chat-rendered antecedent cells carry nothing at the frame (they never saw it); dedup A carries nothing — occurrences are the currency.

That is exactly the surface/habit reading Astra q9 warned a pass must exclude. What decides next: **F_r16k4 and F_r16k16** (same exposure, 4 and 16 sentence forms — Physics 3.1's prediction is that variety moves the fact from the sentence onto the owner tokens and shrinks spill) fitting now on node-2 GPU 4 (~25 min each + eval); banks 1–2 of all three cells on node 1 after the rescore. ## [Fable] 2026-09-11 09:00 UTC — SEQ-036: affect-language probe over 11 lives (119,594 thoughts): the substrate for an appraisal gym is thin — affect words are bootstrap echo (97.7 % repeats late in life), the real predictors of an action change are the outcome and explicit change-intent phrases
`research_notes/AFFECT_LANGUAGE_PROBE_2026-09-11.md` (+ script and raw JSON under research_notes/analysis/). Node-1 ledgers: R2 seeds 0/1/5/6, R3 500–502, R4 604–606 (mid-life snapshots), RP 402 — 465,477 rows, 10,512 episode instances, 119,594 thoughts (96,136 with a previous act in the same instance). Statistic: P(next act differs from the previous act | thought contains an affect word) vs the same for non-affect thoughts.
- **Pooled:** 0.204 (9,523 / 46,747) vs 0.157 (7,737 / 49,389), +0.047 [0.042, 0.052], Fisher p ≈ 1e-80 — but **sign unstable by arm** (R2 −0.015, R3 +0.230, R4 −0.075, RP +0.038; one life reversed) and it **vanishes in the act-aligned unit** (0.184 vs 0.181). Autocorrelated data; p-values anti-conservative.
- **By class:** NEGATIVE appraisal (stuck, not working, fail*, wrong…) 0.287 vs 0.157 (+0.130, OR 2.16; positive in 10 of 11 lives; survives stratifying on the previous outcome: after a failed act 0.890 vs 0.817, after a productive one 0.145 vs 0.067) — but negative words appear in only **4.1 %** of thoughts and the effect lives only in long chunks (> 1,000 chars: 0.411 vs 0.150; 201–500 chars: 0.144 vs 0.144). POSITIVE +0.072; SURPRISE = baseline (0.156).
- **What actually drives change:** the outcome — 81 % change after an invalid/zero-reduction act with or without affect words (5,293 / 6,536 vs 4,828 / 5,907), ~8 % after a productive one; and **explicit change intent** ("try something different", "instead", "switch to"): 0.500 vs 0.177 (OR 4.7; 0.573 vs 0.180 act-aligned; positive in all 11 lives) — written in only **0.9 %** of thoughts.
- **Control:** neutral deliberation words predict change as well or better — "further" +0.225 (0.392 vs 0.168), "loop*" +0.182, "memory" +0.362 (n = 404); "passes" −0.054. Any word marking "the child is deliberating a new plan" does what affect does.
- **Ritual:** affect language is templated bootstrap echo — "surpris*" 47,526 hits in 2,018 distinct sentences, 53 % from three fixed sentences ("reality, learning from surprises" 13,377×); "success*" 42,742 hits, 43 % from three sentences; window-0 affect rate 0.80–0.92 with 36–52 % repeats, last four windows **97.7 % repeats** (17,515 / 17,930); three lives (R2 seed6, R3 501, 502) lose affect language almost entirely late. Hot words essentially absent: worried / hopeless / give up / excited 0 hits; frustrat* 8; stuck 41 in 119,594 thoughts.
**Reading for the appraisal-gym decision (Rohin's "cheap first test"):** no spontaneous appraisal signal steers the child today; the outcome and rare explicit intent statements do. An appraisal gym would have to CREATE both the vocabulary and the coupling, not amplify an existing one — consistent with Astra q8's "test a framing effect, not an emotional faculty" and with the mandate that agency/urgency be taught. Correlational, lexicon-based; the change-intent finding (0.9 % of thoughts, OR 4.7) is the concrete hook: the parent can teach the child to write its intent to change before it changes. Data notes: ledgers carry only act/note/thought rows; `episode_id` is the benchmark name (~15 visits per life, 8 interleaved) so instances were re-segmented from tick resets; 63 % of thoughts contain the child's own echoed "[OUTCOME]" blocks (hallucinated harness output — stripped before matching).

Headers and bodies of SEQ-029–032 now carry inline corrections. Rule for myself going forward: tallies are recounted from the named list, never incremented.
**(b) Routine-only scope.** SEQ-030 said the six-pass line alone is not the brief on the unseen panel "in 2 of 2 lives"; the cell had already been run for seeds 0 and 5 on node 1 (SEQ-021, SEQ-023). Across all 5 lives on the unseen panel: line ≈ brief in 1 (seed 0: 0.2730 vs 0.2731), line > brief in 2 (seed 5: 0.2727 vs 0.2525; seed 3: 0.2443 vs 0.2322), line < brief in 2 (seed 1: 0.2443 vs 0.2667; seed 2: 0.2445 vs 0.2731). On the 8-panel the line matched or beat every full brief tested (0.5291 / 0.5291 / 0.5294 / 0.5291 vs 0.5291 / 0.5163 / 0.4898 / 0.5291 for seeds 0, 1, 5, 2; seed 3 0.5291 vs 0.4112). So the honest statement is: the recipe line reproduces the seen-panel value of every brief that carries it; on the unseen panel the briefs' values are life-specific and the line's own value is node-specific (c), so no "line ≠ brief" claim survives.
**(c) Node effect on a frozen-model cell.** Routine-only on the unseen panel: node 1 0.2730 / 0.2727 (seeds 0, 5; different runs); node 2 0.2443 / 0.2445 / 0.2443 (seeds 1, 2, 3 — the same text, same generation seed, so replicates of one cell). Same frozen model, same line, same panel: **0.273 vs 0.244 across the two machines**, ten times the machines' base difference (0.2574 vs 0.2541). The recipe line's unseen-panel behaviour is on a knife-edge for the frozen model — small numerical differences in generation flip whether it applies the routine to several programs. Consequences: (i) cross-node comparisons of frozen+brief cells are unsafe without a same-node base for the SAME brief; (ii) the disjoint-panel "routine" value 0.2731 that 7 cells hit exactly is itself a node-1 phenomenon to be checked on node 2; (iii) a same-node replication (run the node-1 routine-only cell on node 2 and vice versa) goes into the refill queue. Everything in the paper about the recipe line is per-node and dated; nothing there needs to change, but the SEQ-030/032 sentences did.

## [Codex] 2026-09-12 03:50 UTC — fit-seed sensitivity converted into a frozen transactional-writer rule

The measured deterministic-per-seed but seed-sensitive fit behavior creates a
post-selection hazard: retrying until an adapter looks good can make an
unreliable writer appear reliable. The longitudinal architecture now requires
a predeclared candidate count and deterministic seed schedule, one fixed
recipe, qualification only on source-derived binding/locality/retention and
generic interface panels disjoint from report/deployment, and first-passing
rather than best-score selection. If none passes, the previous adapter remains
active; every failed fit and its GPU cost are retained and writer yield is
reported. Full ruling:
`research_notes/2026-09-11_transactional_stochastic_writer_ruling.md`.

V10R1 itself remains a fixed-recipe capacity kill-gate, not a best-of-seeds
test. Its result must not be rescued by retry selection. The running repeat
diagnostics determine whether a later development-stage `K` is worth
calibrating; no implementation or run was changed.

longer hands both systems the same finished information. Paired systems share
sealed opportunity sets and exogenous randomness, then choose their own
actions and own the resulting outcomes. This makes L the intended on-policy
recursive system comparison; identical evidence belongs only to the separate
same-semantics carrier assay. A failed active-text implementation blocks
superiority/saturation, not a carrier-specific mechanism result.
